`lake build`: Build completed successfully (3960 jobs). No `sorry`, `admit` or custom axioms.

Summary: 2488 theorems in 105 modules: all depend only on propext, Classical.choice,
Quot.sound (7 on none). No theorem uses sorryAx or any other axiom, and no `axiom`
is declared in the CausalSeed modules.

Scope: every `theorem`/`lemma` constant whose defining module is CausalSeed.* in the
environment of `import CausalSeed.Main` (all project modules are imported by Main).
Excluded as auto-generated: internal names (`_proof_`, `match_`, ...), equation lemmas
(`eq_N`, `eq_def`), `sizeOf_spec`, `inj`/`injEq`, `brecOn`/`below` lemmas, and instances.
Axiom name `choice` below means Classical.choice.

Reproduce (from the repository root, after `lake build`):

    lake env lean scripts/AxiomAudit.lean

It prints `<module> <theorem> : [axioms]` for each theorem and a final TOTAL line.
